# Graph Report - L-0713-refresh  (2026-10-09)

## Corpus Check
- 1631 files · ~3,924,014 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 94 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .mmd 38, (none) 34, .jsonl 4)

## Summary
- 39199 nodes · 89460 edges · 1360 communities (1152 shown, 208 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8696 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `04f376ff`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
